- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **The capability roster offered in the UI is exactly the roster the provider declared** — `apps/web/tests/declared-reasoning.e2e.ts:80-85` (`toEqual(['Default','Off','High','Max'])` — minimal/low/medium/xhigh are absent because they were not declared), with the declaration being a map from selectable level to wire spelling and a valueless `off` meaning "supported, send nothing" (`:36-39`, `:52`). Why it matters: August exposes reasoning-effort levels per model; offering a level the provider does not accept is a silent failure at request time.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A model's chosen effort is a first-class part of the deployment default, stored beside provider and model** — `apps/web/tests/declared-reasoning.e2e.ts:109-116` (`reasoningEffort: high` in the profile patch; the trigger's aria-label becomes `选择模型，当前 Acme Think，推理等级 High`). Why it matters: the effort is part of the prompt cache prefix, so it belongs in the same durable default record the provider/model live in.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A session's model route is frozen at creation; only later sessions follow the default** — `apps/web/tests/default-model.e2e.ts:530-534` (a session created after the switch uses the new route; the session that already logged a route keeps deriving from its own log). Why it matters: this is the prefix-stability rule in one sentence — changing the deployment default must not retroactively change a running conversation's cached prefix.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Removing a provider must not retain its profile** — `apps/web/tests/default-model.e2e.ts:542-563` ("A merge update would retain the removed profile"). Why it matters: August's provider store has the same merge-vs-replace hazard; the test asserts the removed route is gone while the other route and the saved selection survive.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Two surfaces for the same choice must be byte-for-byte consistent** — `apps/web/tests/default-model.e2e.ts:202-251` (the button menu and the slash-command popup must share provider order, fuzzy-result order and sticky-header material, asserted by running the same helpers against both). Why it matters: if August offers a model picker in more than one place, this is the cheapest drift detector.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Two pickers must show the same permission vocabulary** — `apps/web/tests/access-confirmation.e2e.ts:300-316` (both the header picker and the `/permission` picker must offer exactly the three standard modes, and the narrow-viewport variant must keep every name readable with `scrollWidth <= clientWidth` at 420 px). Why it matters: a permission ladder shown in two places with different vocabularies is a security bug, not a UI bug.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A capability the host will refuse must not be offered at all** — `apps/web/tests/agent-preset-selection.e2e.ts:1-9` (the session header names the composition but offers no control, "because the host answers `agent-preset-locked` to anything else") and `:427-429` (the header must contain `Minimal mode` as text and must **not** contain `button "Minimal mode"`). Why it matters: a knob the host will reject is a lie to the user; August's UI should surface only controls the host actually honours.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A mode that fails to start is omitted from the picker but keeps its diagnostics** — `apps/web/tests/agent-preset-selection.e2e.ts:312-318` (the refusing preset is not a menuitem, but `agentPresets.resolve(id).broken` still contains `'this row refuses to start'`). Why it matters: August's modes and plugins can fail to compose; hide them from users, keep them debuggable for operators.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A composition change must re-read everything derived from it** — `apps/web/tests/agent-preset-selection.e2e.ts:320-358` (switching to `minimal` must remove the project skill and `plan` from the slash catalog and restore them on switching back; the client's own `model` command stays outside every preset). Why it matters: August's slash catalog, tool set and memory layers are all composition-derived; a stale catalog after a switch is a correctness bug, and this test is the recipe for catching it.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Read the applied state from the HOST, not from the UI** — `apps/web/tests/agent-preset-selection.e2e.ts:190-212` (`livePreset` posts `session/list` over the RPC envelope and reads the projection) and `apps/web/tests/agent-preset-authoring.e2e.ts:90-105` (the same for `agentPreset: 'cordis'`). Why it matters: August's Settings panels must be gated on what the backend actually persisted, not on what the button renders.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Reconnect must not reload the page, lose the draft, or skip the baseline** — `apps/web/tests/connection-recovery.e2e.ts:67-87` (three consecutive WebSocket losses with code 1012; zero navigations; the draft survives; a fresh `session/follow` snapshot arrives each time). Why it matters: August's desktop and web clients both live on this connection; the "keep the mounted application across a transport loss" contract is a rare, high-value regression net.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A transport loss must be re-baselined, not resumed from a stale cursor** — `apps/web/tests/connection-recovery.e2e.ts:34-48` (the route counts `snapshot` frames per `session/follow` streamId). Why it matters: after a reconnect the client must re-derive its cursor from a baseline rather than replaying from an old position, and the test proves the baseline arrived.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A destructive action needs a deliberate second press, and the armed state is observable** — `apps/web/tests/background-job-list.e2e.ts:116-120` (`data-kill-state` goes to `'armed'` on the first click; the kill happens on the second). Why it matters: August's stop/kill controls should be two-press with an observable armed state, so an accidental click cannot terminate a run.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A background job must be owned by the exact live Agent instance, and the job id must be discoverable from the tool's own output** — `apps/web/tests/background-job-list.e2e.ts:70-74` and `:98-100` (the id is parsed out of the bash result with `/\bbash-[0-9a-f]{16}\b/`, not fetched from the registry). Why it matters: August's job registry is keyed by agent; a second agent instance for the same session would orphan the job. The regex-discovery trick also lets a scenario exercise the real path end to end.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A permission denial must be enforced by the host, not by the absence of a button** — `apps/web/tests/approval-composer.e2e.ts:79-105` (a passive WebSocket subscriber with `interactions=none` is refused with `gateway/interactions-forbidden`). Why it matters: August's approval door should reject a non-interactive subscriber at the transport, and the test proves it does rather than assuming the UI was the only door.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **An approval must be answerable by pointer and by keyboard, and produce the same decided event** — `apps/web/tests/approval-composer.e2e.ts:35` (the scenario runs as both `button` and `keyboard`) and `:152-168` (the `approval/decided` event must contain `allowed-once` in both cases). Why it matters: August's approval surface will be driven by a keyboard user and by an automation; the decision path must be one path.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A pre-approval screen must not leak the arguments it is still waiting for** — `apps/web/tests/changed-files-turn.e2e.ts:159-178` (the preparing row shows the path but has no expandable region, no `<pre>` and no size badge). Why it matters: August shows "preparing" rows while a tool's arguments stream; the partial arguments are not a stable contract and must not be rendered as if they were.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Per-turn change summaries must survive git-ignore** — `apps/web/tests/changed-files-turn.e2e.ts:194-199` (the ignored `app.local`'s counts come from the write call, not from git) and `:445` (its comparison comes from copies captured around the write call). Why it matters: August's "what did this turn change" card would silently miss ignored files if it only consulted git; capturing the before/after copies around each write is the fallback.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A per-session summary of what the turn did must be served by the host for the announcing event, not stored in the log** — `apps/web/tests/changed-files-turn.e2e.ts:187-193` ("the log carries only the turn; the Host serves the summary for the announcing event while the Session lives"). Why it matters: it keeps the durable log small and lets the summary be recomputed — a good shape for August's per-turn artifacts.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A retry must have a backoff, an identical body, and must live in page state rather than storage** — `apps/web/tests/bonus-notice.e2e.ts:466-489` (the first retry gap must be ≥ 900 ms and every attempt's body must be byte-identical) and `:512-514` (displaying and acknowledging writes no localStorage key). Why it matters: August's durable queues need the same shape — a retry you can prove waited, with a body that did not drift, and a pending state that is not mistaken for persisted state.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A notification must be acknowledged only after it was shown, and showing it must be free of side effects** — `apps/web/tests/bonus-notice.e2e.ts:296-313` and `:497-521`. Why it matters: August's user-facing notices (limits, quota, permission denials) should be ack-after-display, and "showing a notice writes nothing" is a testable privacy property.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Persist a draft as structured data with explicit references, and treat the transition matrix as the contract** — `apps/web/tests/draft-initialization.e2e.ts:19-32` (the `DraftSnapshot` shape with `offset`/`length`/`ref`/`label`/`appearance`) and `:618-653` (the nine-row transition matrix, each row asserted individually with an optional reload). Why it matters: August's composer must survive reloads, workspace switches and overlapping creations; a structured draft plus an explicit transition table turns "what happens when…" from folklore into a table.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **When two creations overlap, only the LATEST target gets the draft** — `apps/web/tests/draft-initialization.e2e.ts:663-754` (both completion orders asserted). Why it matters: the classic stale-async-response bug; the "latest wins" rule needs a test in both interleavings.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A late-arriving catalog must re-match the draft without touching text, selection or undo history** — `apps/web/tests/draft-initialization.e2e.ts:470-568` (the exact selection offsets are captured, the catalog is released, and the selection plus the chip's DOM identity are asserted unchanged, then Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Shift+Z are exercised). Why it matters: August's skills/memory catalog arrives asynchronously; re-matching must not eat the user's undo stack.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A late-arriving replay must not mutate its own source** — `apps/web/tests/collapse-timing.e2e.ts:186` (`expect(await readFile(SEED, 'utf8')).toBe(fixture)`) and the equivalent fixture-inventory assertions (`assertFixtureInventory`) used across the suite. Why it matters: cheap and catches a whole class of "the test rewrote the recording" bugs.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **The persisted log, not the DOM, is the ground truth for a turn's lifecycle** — `apps/web/tests/collapse-timing.e2e.ts:173-177` (the session's `turn/end` events must be exactly the two expected) and `apps/web/tests/chat-continuous-conversation.e2e.ts:266-295` (per-turn event-shape assertions: one `turn/start`, one user message, one final assistant message, one `turn/end` with `reason: { kind: 'completed' }`, and an exact expanded-chunk count). Why it matters: August's turn accounting is what billing, compaction and the stop badge read; asserting the event stream makes those consumers safe.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A prefix that must stay cache-stable must be asserted as stable** — `apps/web/tests/chat-continuous-conversation.e2e.ts:341-342` (`request/header` must appear exactly once with reason `initial` across twelve turns) and `apps/web/tests/cot-translation.e2e.ts:356-359` (the recorded header, derived history and request header must be byte-identical after the plugin wrote its own records). Why it matters: an August turn that rewrites the request header or the derived messages destroys provider-side prefix caching; this is the assertion that catches it.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A perf harness must report numbers but assert only structure** — `apps/web/tests/complex-history.perf.ts:1-4` ("host speed is not a correctness contract; structural assertions keep the number of workspaces and history entries from silently shrinking") with the retained-state probe at `:539-551` (force a GC, then read DOM elements, nodes, listeners and heap). Why it matters: August's own harness can adopt this for long-session and soak work: never gate on milliseconds, always gate on counts, and measure leaks by forcing a GC.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Measure the main thread under a reasoning storm and set a budget** — `apps/web/stress-tests/reasoning-chunks.stress.ts:21` (`MAIN_THREAD_DELAY_BUDGET_MS = 250`), `:129` (100,000 reasoning chunks) and `:222-225` (both the max tick delay and the scheduled-interaction delay must stay under the budget). Why it matters: reasoning/thinking content is exactly what DeepSeek models stream most heavily; 100k small chunks through the real carrier with a 250 ms budget is a directly transferable gate for August's reasoning renderer.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A client-plugin failure must stay local to the page** — `apps/web/tests/client-plugin-live.e2e.ts:181-205` (the fiber state is failed, the Host's enablement is unchanged, and the page offers a "retry this page's sync" button). Why it matters: August's client-plugin/HMR system should degrade to a per-page retry rather than disabling the feature on the Host.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A bootstrap module cannot be swapped without a reload, and the page must say so instead of crashing** — `apps/web/tests/client-plugin-live.e2e.ts:249-282` (`replacing bootstrap module … requires a page reload`, with the settings page's own input still connected). Why it matters: August's boot graph has the same bootstrap tier; an in-page, legible refusal beats a half-booted UI.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Enforce "no experimental package in the shipped bundle" as a build fact** — `apps/web/product-isolation.ts:30-104` (`WebProductBundleIsolation` captures chunk, asset, CSS and worker inputs and verifies them in a `generateBundle` post-hook) and `:12-22` (a dependency-analysis build must have `build.write === false`). Why it matters: August can use the same input census to keep experimental features out of a shipped build, and the `write: false` rule makes a read-only analysis build incapable of emitting anything by accident.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Fail the build rather than silently shipping a fallback asset** — `apps/web/katex-fonts.ts:20-25` (a font face with no WOFF2 source throws). Why it matters: the same rule applies to August's own model/profile assets — a missing variant should stop the build, not downgrade quietly.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Sandbox the renderer and make the sandbox attribute live-observable** — `apps/web/tests/document-preview.e2e.ts:838-899` (the iframe's `sandbox` is `''` with coding view off and `allow-scripts` with it on, and it flips back immediately when the setting is turned off) and `:881-883` (even with `allow-scripts` the frame cannot touch its parent — `SecurityError`). Why it matters: August renders HTML in previews; the capability should be visible in the DOM and revoked the moment the setting changes.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Sanitize untrusted HTML down to "no hrefs, no iframes, no requests"** — `apps/web/tests/document-preview.e2e.ts:815-832` (ordinary links, SVG `<a>`, `<math href>`, declarative shadow roots, `<set>`, `<animate>` and `<iframe>` all removed, and `expect(blockedRequests).toEqual([])`). Why it matters: the test asserts the *absence* of escape vectors rather than the presence of a sanitizer — a much stronger and more portable contract.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **Assert the ABSENCE of leaked content, not just its presence** — `apps/web/tests/auto-review-denial.e2e.ts:86` / `:111` (`hidden-input` must not appear), `apps/web/tests/deepseek-messages-settings.e2e.ts:73-77` (the secret must not be in the profile patch, must be in the credential store, and must not be in the rendered page), and `apps/web/tests/cot-translation-paid.e2e.ts:283` (the fixture token must not appear anywhere in the persisted log). Why it matters: August handles provider keys, tokens and user content; "the secret is absent from here" is a testable property, and the credential store is the only place it should live.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A credential must be written to the credential store, never to the profile** — `apps/web/tests/deepseek-messages-settings.e2e.ts:66-77` (`cordis.patch.yml` contains `id: llm-deepseek` but not `sk-e2e-`; `.credentials.yaml` contains `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY: sk-e2e-messages` and not `DEEPSEEK_MESSAGES_API_KEY`). Why it matters: the same split applies to August's provider keys and service tokens, and the test proves the wrong store was not written to.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A locale preference lives in the Host, not in browser storage** — `apps/web/tests/desktop-locale.e2e.ts:61` (the OS languages are not saved) and `:69-81` (after an explicit choice the profile patch contains `preference: en` and `localStorage['dsh.locale']` is still `null`). Why it matters: August's desktop and web clients must not diverge on language; the durable choice is the Host's.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A session's plan/tool state must survive a reload and be editable** — `apps/web/tests/cold-blank-session.e2e.ts:26-40` and `:55-74` (a pending inbox row written directly to persistence is shown on open, survives a reload, and can be edited and removed). Why it matters: August's queue must survive a crash; a directly-written persistence event is the closest thing to a crash-recovery test.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A blank session's writer contention must have a defined resolution** — `apps/web/tests/blank-writer-reuse.e2e.ts:63-91` (reuse the selected blank; if its writer is held, create a new one rather than trying another blank; the attached-session count is asserted exactly). Why it matters: August's multi-window / multi-agent cases will hit the same contention; "never silently try a different blank" is a rule worth having.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A recorded preset that the deployment no longer declares must fall back to the default AND record the fallback** — `apps/web/tests/blank-preset-reuse.e2e.ts:91-103` and `:115-125` (`agent-preset/selected` with `agentPreset: 'standard'` is persisted). Why it matters: August's mode registry will evolve; the fallback must be durable so the next cold resume is stable and the user is not re-prompted.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **A permission/preset switch must be visible in the UI only when it is actually in effect** — `apps/web/tests/auto-review-denial.e2e.ts:93-95` (the access-mode control's aria-label is read from the shipped projection) and `apps/web/tests/agent-preset-selection.e2e.ts:427-429` (a label, not a button). Why it matters: August's access mode is the single most consequential indicator in the UI; it must be a read of the host's state, not a local toggle.
- **[dsh-apps-web#1]** - **An ignorable record type keeps plugin data out of the model's history** — `apps/web/tests/cot-translation.e2e.ts:31-35` (`pluginRecordOf(event)?.type === 'plugin:translator/request' | 'plugin:translator/result'`) with the records marked `ignorable: true` (`:261-269`). Why it matters: August's memory/skills writes need a first-class "this is durable but not model-facing" event kind. ---
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **Surface the provider cache-hit rate next to the token totals, and a context-used meter beside it** — `apps/web/tests/expected/cordis-history/ui.expected.md:73` (`button "66.8K tok · Cache hit 77%"`) and `apps/web/tests/expected/skill-user-invoke/ui.expected.md:27-28` (`272 tok · Cache hit 0%` / `0% of context used`). August's own notes admit it freezes the boot memory index *specifically to protect the provider prefix cache* — an invisible optimisation with no observable readout. A one-line footer carrying `tokens · Cache hit N%` turns "we think the prefix is stable" into a measured fact that a regression in prompt assembly would break, and `N% of context used` gives the user a compaction trigger before the window is full.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **The send button unlocks only after the staged-attachment receipt lands, and a failed submission dumps the feedback regions and the event-type sequence** — `apps/web/tests/file-upload-round.e2e.ts:276-293`. August's `write_file` and its edit-verify gate are the same class of problem: the model must never be asked to act on a file the host has not yet made durable. The second half matters more — the failure throws `submission retained N draft cards; feedback=[…]; events=…`, which is a debugging affordance, not just an assertion. Transfer it to August's staged-edit / staged-upload doors.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **The "authorised suffix" release contract: the collector may see the log up to and including the consent event, and nothing after** — `apps/web/tests/feedback-release.e2e.ts:66-85` (the `expectFeedbackRelease` helper computes `authorized = events.filter(e => e.seq <= boundary.seq)` and asserts the collector received exactly that), and the negative twin at `:206-217` (command tails, provider changes and browser reloads must release nothing, proved by the last persisted event's `seq` being *greater* than the last authorised one). August has no equivalent of "only ship what the user consented to ship" for feedback, session logs, or memory exports. This is a mechanically checkable privacy boundary expressed as a prefix, and it composes with a `seq`-ordered event log for free.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **A "the same model reviews every native tool call *and every inner call*" permission mode, with the refusal escalated to the human** — `apps/web/tests/expected/access-confirmation/auto-confirmation.expected.md:4`. Three details transfer: the reviewer is the *acting* model (no separate critic model to prompt), the review happens at the **inner** call boundary and not merely at the outer tool boundary, and a review refusal becomes a human decision rather than a dropped call. August's tool-boundary guard (`hooks/secret_guard.py`) is a static policy; this is a dynamic one.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **IME-safe Enter, with the `compositionend + 9ms` case pinned** — `apps/web/tests/expected/plugin-manager/ime-enter.expected.md:2-22` (five composition shapes all with `inspect=0, install=0; editable; value retained`; only a plain Enter at `:22` performs the action). The 9ms case is the one that bites: a naive `isComposing` check fires the submit immediately after `compositionend`, which on a CJK IME still lands mid-composition. August runs on Windows with a CJK-primary user, and the composer's Enter-to-send path is exactly the door this covers.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **A per-model reasoning-effort TIER TABLE with per-tier wire values, not a flat enum** — `apps/web/tests/expected/models-settings/declared-edit.expected.md:62-88` (three tier comboboxes each with its own `API 参数值` textbox and a delete button) and `apps/web/tests/expected/models-settings/image-only.expected.md:62-88` where the semantic tier `high` carries the wire value **`deep`**. August's per-model configuration today is flat fields (`priceInPerM`, `maxTools`, `toolSurface`); a reasoning-capable gateway needs *semantic tier → provider-specific wire value*, because `high` on one provider is `xhigh` or `deep` on another. The table shape also makes "remove a tier" a first-class operation.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **A stale-configuration acknowledgement gate that blocks saving until the human re-reads the changed bindings** — `apps/web/tests/expected/shortcuts/edit-stale.expected.md:1-5` (`Restore default` `[disabled]` + a notice + an `I have reviewed the latest configuration` button). This is the generic "the world changed under your form" pattern. August's brain-config keys and its Settings panel are exactly this shape — the backend's accepted key set and ranges can move under a form the user already filled in — and a silent save of a stale form is how a key ends up written that the backend rejects.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **Archiving a session with live work enumerates exactly what will be stopped, by process** — `apps/web/tests/expected/session-archive-active/dialog.expected.md:5-7` (a `Work that will be stopped` list containing "The turn in progress" and the literal `node -e "…"` background-job command). August's session archive and its bot-DM ceilings both leave "what happens to work in flight" ambiguous; naming the actual process is what makes a destructive action informed rather than scary.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **The golden refresh is the ONLY writer; a missing golden fails with a healing command** — the mechanism lives at `apps/web/tests/scaffold.ts:1710-1720` and is used by every one of the 229 expected files in this shard (e.g. `apps/web/tests/feedback-command.e2e.ts:87`). The property that transfers is not the snapshot format but the **one-writer rule**: `DSH_SNAPSHOT=refresh` is the only code path that may write a golden, and a missing golden in replay mode is a hard error naming the exact command. August's `check:*` gates are static and cannot catch "the UI changed"; a golden corpus with a single writer and a healing command can.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **Bind the recorded fixture to the driving script by exact equality on the user prompt** — `apps/web/tests/feedback-command.e2e.ts:56-59`, repeated at `apps/web/tests/feedback-release.e2e.ts:159-162` and `apps/web/tests/file-upload-round.e2e.ts:225-228` (`expect(fixtureUserPrompts(await readFile(FIXTURE, 'utf8'))).toEqual([PROMPT])`). The recorded model output and the script that drives the browser are two artefacts that drift apart silently; asserting the exact prompt list makes a re-record fail loudly instead of quietly changing the question the test is asking.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **A fixture-inventory gate that also asserts the committed JSONL is a fixed point of the prompt-bulk scrubber and the identity redactor** — used at `apps/web/tests/feedback-command.e2e.ts:99-103` and `apps/web/tests/file-upload-round.e2e.ts:431-437`; the implementation is `apps/web/tests/scaffold.ts:1728-1755`. This is a gate on the **test corpus**, not on the code: the directory must contain exactly the named files, and every committed session log must be unchanged by `scrubModelRequestBulk` (no prompt text, no tool-schema bulk) and by `redactSessionSnapshotIds` (no unredacted identities). August removed its test suite; this is the kind of self-audit a repo without tests is most exposed to.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **A console tripwire that treats "gap repair" and "discontinuous" as failures, and forgives only the connection-loss warning produced by an intentional reload** — `apps/web/tests/scaffold.ts:1773-1796` (`watchConsole` / `acknowledgeReloadConnectionLoss`), asserted empty at `apps/web/tests/feedback-command.e2e.ts:95-96`. August's SSE reader consumes a live stream; a gap repair is a silent discontinuity in the loop's view of the world, and "eventual consistency" is exactly the excuse that hides it. Making the wire's health a test tripwire is how you make it visible in production too.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **A route-only adapter that throws if a scenario that declared no model call actually issues one** — `apps/web/tests/scaffold.ts:259-264` (`stream()` throws "a model call was issued by a scenario that declared no replay fixture") and `:899-909` (the route-only registration for a UI scenario that needs provider labels but must never call a model). A UI test that accidentally triggers a model turn should **fail**, not quietly cost money and pass. August's UI-only harnesses would benefit from the same "declared no model calls ⇒ any model call is a failure" contract.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **Express a hang/throw scenario as a chunk script via a replay override, not as a recorded session** — `apps/web/tests/expected/steer-all/replay.override.json:1-45` (block-start / reasoning-delta / tool-call-delta / block-end / usage / finish chunks for two turns). It lets the steering surface be tested without a real model, by scripting the exact wire chunks — including a tool call the model would never make. August's turn-boundary and stall scenarios currently need a live provider or a bespoke fake.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **Measure hover choreography as a named-stage state machine instead of a screenshot** — `apps/web/tests/expected/session-row-hover/grouped.expected.json:1-40` (ten named stages proving the reveal is exclusive, survives a portal round trip and cools down) and `apps/web/tests/expected/session-row-hover/actions.expected.json:1-37` (the tooltip appears only after a dwell and clears on return to the title). A hover-only affordance is a trap if it lingers or if two rows reveal at once; asserting the *sequence* of states makes the timing reviewable in a diff.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **One model-label table drives the composer trigger, the `/model` picker and the command-picker group headers** — `apps/web/tests/expected/default-model/model-labels.expected.md:1-42` (provider / model / name / **slot** / inline). The `slot` key is the useful part: it names *which* surface the label belongs to, so a label can be shared without the two surfaces drifting. August's own notes record that `getProvidersAsModels()` and `_provider_to_dict` rebuild model entries field by field and a new per-model field must be named in **both** or it is silently dropped — a table with explicit slots is the fix for that class of bug.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **Let the model declare its context window and output cap, accepting both a human-formatted default and a literal override** — `apps/web/tests/expected/onboarding-deepseek-config/models.expected.md:37-43` (context window typed literally as `131072`, output cap `64K`) against the default catalogue's `1M` / `256K` placeholders at `apps/web/tests/expected/onboarding-deepseek-config/default-models.expected.md:36-41`. August's `contextWindow` / `defaultMaxTokens` come from the provider catalogue today; a per-model override that is editable in the UI is what lets a gateway advertise a real window instead of a guess.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **The output-limit notice names the recovery verb and promises the earlier output survived** — `apps/web/tests/expected/max-tokens-notice/history-turn.expected.txt:1-3` ("The reply was cut off; earlier output is preserved in the conversation. Send \"continue\" to let the model resume."). August records `turn_end {reason: 'length'}` and renders an amber stop-reason badge, but the *contract with the user* — that a specific word resumes and that nothing was lost — is the transferable half.
- **[dsh-apps-web#2]** - **Measure the browser-facing geometry as *relations*, never as pixels** — `apps/web/tests/expected/composer-tab-geometry/geometry.expected.md:1-40` (a Chat-vs-Trajectory control that removes the scrollbar-gutter compensation in the page and shows the card then moving 2.5px, against the production 0px), and `apps/web/tests/expected/markdown-wide-table/geometry.expected.md:1-21` (fill / wide / long-cell relations at three widths, plus the "wrap-first engagement" assertion). The counterfactual block is the key idea: the golden proves the *cause* of the 0px, not just the 0px. ---
